Window: apps first found between 2026-11-16 19:04 UTC (20:04 Europe/Warsaw) and 2026-11-17 19:49 UTC (20:49 Europe/Warsaw) and released since 2026-11-15 19:04 UTC (20:04 Europe/Warsaw), plus apps found earlier whose release date arrived in that span. Date-only release dates count by calendar day in America/Los_Angeles. Storefronts: us.
